### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP は保証せず速さを優先する**点です。

TCP はデータを確実に届けるために再送や順序整理を行うので遅れやすいですが、Web や メールのように欠けると困る通信に向いています。UDP は再送などをせず軽いので、動画配信やオンラインゲームのようにリアルタイム性が大事な通信に使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama privada al día con la base y dejar el historial lineal antes de compartirla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar tal cual cómo ocurrieron los cambios, porque rebase reescribe commits y no conviene hacerlo sobre ramas que otros ya usan.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接的，保证数据完整有序地送到；UDP 是不可靠的、无连接的，只管快速发出去，不保证到达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to replay it onto the latest main for a clean, linear history. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rebasing rewrites commits that others may have built on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接给文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配所有路径里包含这段文字的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果只想跑文件里的某一个测试，用 `-t` 按测试名过滤：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "某个测试的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data files. If the system crashes midway, the database replays that log on restart to finish or undo the changes, so committed data survives and partial writes don't corrupt anything.
```

--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着改 compaction 那个 PR 的锁粒度，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找出 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把 compaction 过程中持有的锁范围缩小，只在真正需要的地方加锁。
- 在同一个分支上继续提交，不要新开 PR，也不要合并。

完成标准：
- 锁粒度的改动已提交到原来的分支。
- 相关测试跑过，把测试输出报告出来。
- 说明每处锁范围是怎么改细的。

不在范围内：
- 不合并，不推送到主分支，不改 PR 的状态（保持 draft）。
- 不动 review 之外的逻辑。

工作目录：沿用 h_compaction_pr 原来的仓库和分支。
```

  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest run</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">No runs yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>Nothing yet. Each night's result lands here.</div>
  </div>

<script>
function render(nights) {
  nights = Array.isArray(nights) ? nights : [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
  var sub = document.getElementById('latest-sub');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  empty.hidden = nights.length > 0;
  if (nights.length) {
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    latest.textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    sub.textContent = last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  }
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = n.night;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · stalls ' + n.stalls;
    main.appendChild(s);
    var v = document.createElement('div');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = n.writes + '/s';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(v);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render);

      <div class="k-field"><label>哪天</label><input class="k-input" id="day" value="第1天"></div>
      <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="打车、午饭、酒店…"></div>

<script>
(function () {
  var items = [];
  var editId = null;

  function $(s) { return document.querySelector(s); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    … ducter.set('items', items);
    resetForm();
    render();
  }

  function del() {
    if (!editId) return;
    items = items.filter(function (it) { return it.id !== editId; });
    ducter.set('items', items);
    resetForm();
    render();
  }

  $('#save').addEventListener('click', save);
  $('#del').addEventListener('click', del);
  $('#cancel').addEventListener('click', resetForm);

  $('#days').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var it = items.filter(function (x) { return x.id === row.getAttribute('data-id'); })[0];
    if (!it) return;
    editId = it.id;
    $('#day').value = it.day;
    $('#what').value = it.what;
    $('#amt').value = it.amt;
    $('#save').textContent = '保存修改';
    $('#del').style.display = '';
    $('#cancel').style.display = '';
    $('#addbox').open = true;
    $('#what').focus();
  });

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(function (v) {
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(function (v) {
      items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

    cat: pick,
    note: $('note').value.trim(),

  });

  $('note').value = '';
  $('err').textContent = '';
  await save();
  render();
};

$('list').onclick = e => {
  const r = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
  if (!r) return;
  sel = sel === r.dataset.id ? null : r.dataset.id;
  render();
};

$('del').onclick = async e => {
  if (e.target.id !== 'rm') return;
  items = items.filter(x => x.id !== sel);
  sel = null;
  await save();
  render();
};

ducter.onChange(async () => {
  const v = await ducter.get('expenses');
  items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
});

init();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>

  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div id="list" class="k-list"></div>
  </div>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-card">
      <div class="k-field">
        <label for="t">时间（如 14:30）</label>
        <input class="k-input" id="t" type="text" placeholder="14:30">
      </div>
      <div class="k-field">
        <label for="m">奶量（ml）</label>

<script>
(function () {
  var feeds = [];

  function pad(n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
  function todayKey() {
    var d = new Date();
    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate());
  }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39 … 没记';
      listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">今天还没有记录，喝一顿就记一笔。</div>';
      return;
    }

    totalEl.textContent = total + ' ml';
    subEl.textContent = list.length + ' 次，最近一次 ' + list[list.length - 1].time;

    listEl.innerHTML = list.map(function (f) {
      return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(f.time) +
        '</div><div class="k-row-value">' + esc(f.ml) + ' ml</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(function (v) {
      feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var time = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
    var ml = document.getElementById('m').value.trim();
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(time) || !(Number(ml) > 0)) return;
    feeds.push({ date: todayKey(), time: time, ml: Number(ml) });
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    document.getElementById('t').value = '';
    document.getElementById('m').value = '';
    render();
    ducter.summary(feeds.filter(function (f) { return f.date === todayKey(); }).length + ' 次');
  });

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs, each with a tap to mark it landed. The count at the top updates as you tick them off.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2118 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
var done = {};

function render() {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  var open = 0;
  items.forEach(function (it) {
    var isDone = !!done[it.id];
    if (!isDone) open++;
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    var t = document.createElement('div');
    t.textContent = it.title;
    t.style.textDecoration = isDone ? 'line-through' : 'none';
    t.style.color = isDone ? 'var(--d-muted)' : 'var(--d-ink)';
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = isDone ? 'Landed' : it.sub;
    main.appendChild(t);
    main.appendChild(s);
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'k-chip';
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isDone ? 'true' : 'false');
    btn.textContent = isDone ? '✓ Landed' : 'Open';
    btn.onclick = function () {
      done[it.id] = !done[it.id];
      if (!done[it.id]) delete done[it.id];
      save();
    };
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(btn);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById('open').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0
    ? 'All landed'
    : open + ' still waiting · ' + (items.length - open) + ' landed';
  ducter.summary(open + ' open');
}

function save() {
  ducter.set('done', done);
  render();
}

Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (v) {
  done = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {};
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(function () {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (v) {
    done = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {};
    render();
  });
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"d412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"m10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"h1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"d418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个 3D 风格的笔记本电脑插画：奶油色机身和键盘，深蓝色的屏幕边框，屏幕是空白的。

适合放在空状态里。空白的屏幕正好表示"这里还没有内容"，画面柔和、不吵，也不会让人觉得出错了。用在"还没有文档""暂无记录"这类页面上很自然。

需要注意两点：一是要和你的界面配色搭得上，奶油色和深蓝色如果跟主色调差太远会显得突兀；二是深色模式下深蓝色边框可能对比不够，要实际看一下，必要时换一版或加个浅色底。
```
